# MK60E5 coding data (NCS Expert, SPdaten `daten`)

Generated by `ncs_decode.py report`. Decoded from the NCS Expert data files in `E89/E89/daten`: SGFAM coding files (`MK60_*.Cxx`), keyword tables `SWTFSW03.dat` / `SWTPSW03.dat` / `SWTASW03.dat`, coding variant table `E89CVT.000`, ECU selection table `E89SGET.000` and the order table `E89AT.000` (option code -> order keyword).

Columns: **Block/Addr/Len/Mask** = location in the DSC coding memory, **Function (FSW)** = coding parameter, **Values** = PSW names and the bytes written, **Selected by** = CVT rule (vehicle-order expression -> PSW). `PL2` is true for every car of this platform.

`DSC_89` / `DSC_84` / `DSC8_RPA` are the Bosch DSC8/DXC8 of the E89 Z4, E84 X1 and xDrive models (SGBD `DSC_89.prg` = "Robert Bosch DXC8 PLUS"), not the MK60E5.

## CAN-relevant parameters

| Parameter | Coding file(s) | Meaning for the CAN interface |
|---|---|---|
| M3_GETRIEBE | MK60_M3.C08 @2/0xC0 | Gearbox: 0 = manual, 2 = M DCT (option 2MK/2TC). Inference: decides whether the EGS frames (e.g. 0x0BA) are expected/supervised |
| RPA | MK60_M3.C08 @3/0x08 | Run-flat/tyre pressure warning (RPA) active unless direct TPMS (option 2VB) is fitted |
| MSA | MK60_M3.C08 @20/0x40 | Auto start-stop (option 1CC). Inference: enables evaluation of PT-CAN 0x308 (STATUS_MSA, DTC D379) |
| C0F_GETRIEBE | MK60_87.C0F @2/0xC0 | Gearbox: 0 = manual, 1 = automatic, 2 = DCT. Inference: decides whether the EGS frames (e.g. 0x0BA, DTC D357) are expected/supervised |
| C0F_RPA | MK60_87.C0F @3/0x08 | Run-flat/tyre pressure warning (RPA) active unless direct TPMS (option 2VB) is fitted |
| C0F_AFS | MK60_87.C0F @10/0x20 | Active Steering fitted (option 217): enables the F-CAN Active Steering frames 0x118/0x11F (RX) and 0x128 (TX, inference) |
| C0F_ECBA_DCC | MK60_87.C0F @18/0xC0 | Cruise control mode: 0 = none, 1 = ACC (option 541, LDM is the cruise controller), 2 = D_GESCHW_REG (option 544, DSC itself is the cruise controller). Inference: value 2 is what makes the DSC transmit 0x0BF/0x193 (TIS: 'DSC takes over LDM functions with option 544') |
| C0F_V_EINHEIT | MK60_87.C0F @19/0x40 | Speed unit for the DSC cruise function (mph when option 544 and no km/h speedometer) |
| C0F_SCHUBABSCHALTUNG | MK60_87.C0F @19/0x80 | Overrun fuel cut-off request by the DSC cruise function (option 544) |
| C0F_MOTOR | MK60_87.C0F @30/0x1F | Engine variant (vehicle model parameter set); no CAN layout change known |
| SZL | MK60_87.C0F @0/0x7F | Steering column switch cluster variant (block 0x3001): 2 = E8x, 4 = E8x + AFS, 5 = E9x, 7 = AFS. Inference: tells the DSC which SZL/steering-angle variant sends F-CAN 0x0C9 |
| C0X_C8 | MK60_87.C0F @0/0x80 | Always 1 on series cars; probably enables reception of F-CAN 0x0C8 (CVT group DSC8_C0E_C8, DTC D373 'message 200 or 201') |
